- **longbridge-ml-strategy**: Machine-learning prediction strategy framework via Longbridge Securities — walk-forward rolling training with feature engineering (MACD, RSI, Bollinger Band width, volume change rate) and a scikit-learn classifier (Random Forest / Gradient Boosting); retrains every 60 days, predicts 5-day direction probability; evaluates win rate, profit factor, and Sharpe ratio.
